Autenticação

All routes require API key in the header Authorization: Bearer mia_xxxxx. It also accepts the api-key. No route requires Premium except custom STW and BR shop images.

How do I get your API key?

  1. Join the Mia Lounge
  2. Run /api crear
  3. Receive your key in a private message
Entrar no Mia Lounge
JavaScript (Axios):

Idiomas

Several STW resources, STW alerts, STW shop and BR shop routes translate in-game names and text. If you don’t set a language, the API responds in es-ES.

Query parameter (use one): locale · lang · idioma

es-ES · default es-LAT es-419 en-US fr-FR it-IT pt-BR pt-PT ja-JP de-DE tr-TR zh-CN

Respostas include "locale": "..." with the applied language.

Planos e limites

Choose the plan that best fits your needs. Limits reset automatically every minute.

Plano gratuito

€0

por mês

  • 180 req/min · 17 JSON routes
  • Suporte da comunidade
POPULAR

Plano Premium

€5

por mês

  • 360 req/min · 19 routes (+ STW and BR images)
  • Personalizadoizable logo and text on the shop image
  • Suporte prioritário

Plano Programadores

Personalizado

contactar

  • Personalizado limits
  • Termos by agreement
  • Suporte dedicado

Request limits: if you exceed the limit, the API will warn you with the corresponding code.

Change plan: you can upgrade your plan anytime from Mia Lounge.

Rotas da API

21 public endpoints · grouped by game mode. Expandir each route to see parameters, response, and examples.

20 routes

No results. Try “stw”, “online”, “gifts”, or a route like /stw/hero.

Conta Epic

Player status and gifts · valid for any Fortnite mode.

2 routes

Conta Epic

Player online

API Key

Check whether a player is online in Fortnite.

Required body { "accountId": "tu_account_id" }
POST https://api.miadsc.xyz/v1/online
Resposta 
{
  "isOnline": false
}
JavaScript (Axios):

Check remaining gifts

API Key Epic token

Returns how many gifts an account has left (max 5 every 24 h).

Required body { "accessToken": "token_oauth_de_epic" }
POST https://api.miadsc.xyz/v1/regalos
Resposta 
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "regalosRestantes": 3,
    "regalosUsados": 2,
    "regalosMaximos": 5,
    "proximoRegaloDisponible": 1716403200000,
    "accountId": "7ab3c9d2e8f14a6b9c0d1e2f3a4b5c6d"
  }
}
JavaScript (Axios):

JavaScript (Axios):

BR shop image

API KeyPremiumLoja

Generates a PNG like /tienda br image, mas com o teu logo, o teu link por baixo do título (discord.gg/...) e o teu support text in the footer. Requires Premium. Use POST with JSON if the text has spaces.

Required parameters logoUrl (http/https logo URL) · headerText (substitui discord.gg/miabot; alias: discord, discordText, promoText) · supportText (image footer text)
Optional parameters locale · lang · idioma
POST https://api.miadsc.xyz/v1/br/tienda/image
JSON body (recommended) 
{
  "logoUrl": "https://cdn.discordapp.com/avatars/.../avatar.png",
  "headerText": "discord.gg/mi-servidor",
  "supportText": "Suporte Mia haciendote premium en miadsc.xyz/pt-PT/premium",
  "locale": "es-ES"
}
GET Also accepts a query string (encode spaces with %20).
Successful response

PNG (Content-Type: image/png) with the full item shop, your logo, your link, and your text.

Error if parameters are missing 
{
  "success": false,
  "error": "parametros_personalizacion_requeridos",
  "message": "You must provide logoUrl, headerText, and supportText to generate the custom image.",
  "requiredParams": {
    "logoUrl": "URL pública http(s) do logótipo (substitui a imagem da Mia).",
    "headerText": "Texto por baixo do título (substitui discord.gg/miabot).",
    "supportText": "Texto do rodapé da imagem (substitui a mensagem de apoio da Mia)."
  }
}

AES keys

Fortnite AES keys

API Key

Returns the main AES key e as dynamic keys for paks (.utoc) for the current Fortnite version. Short cache on the API; use force=1 to force a refresh.

Optional parameters force / refresh (1 o true to bypass cache)
GET https://api.miadsc.xyz/v1/fortnite/aes

JavaScript (Axios):

STW shop image

API KeyPremiumSTWLoja

Generates a PNG como a de /tienda stw, mas com o teu logo e o teu support text instead of Mia’s image and the "Suporte Mia" message. Requires Premium. Use POST with JSON if the text has spaces.

Required parameters logoUrl (http/https logo URL) · supportText (image footer text)
Optional parameters section (rotational · semanal, special · eventos) · subtitle · accountIconUrl · locale
POST https://api.miadsc.xyz/v1/stw/tienda/image
JSON body (recommended) 
{
  "logoUrl": "https://cdn.discordapp.com/avatars/.../avatar.png",
  "supportText": "me pica la polla",
  "section": "rotational"
}
GET Also accepts a query string (encode spaces with %20).
Successful response

PNG (Content-Type: image/png) with the item grid, your logo, and your text.

Error if parameters are missing 
{
  "success": false,
  "error": "parametros_personalizacion_requeridos",
  "message": "Debes indicar logoUrl y supportText to generate the custom image.",
  "requiredParams": {
    "logoUrl": "URL pública http(s) do logótipo (substitui a imagem da Mia).",
    "supportText": "Texto do rodapé da imagem (substitui a mensagem de apoio da Mia)."
  }
}
